- **kelly-criterion**: Kelly Criterion (John L. Kelly Jr., 1956) — the betting-size formula that maximizes long-run asset growth rate. Fractional Kelly (1/2, 1/4) dampens volatility. - **kelpie**: Default Herdr transport for inter-agent send, message, tell, ask, notify, or reply (HERDR_ENV=1). Also use whenever Kelpie is named: start, adopt, pending, cancel, retire, recover, and notices over the local socket. Read it before waiting on an answer another agent owes you, when a &lt;kelpie …&gt; envelope arrives, and whenever you are blocked on someone else — waiting in-session is the mistake this skill exists to prevent. ([dcadenas/kelpie](https://github.com/dcadenas/kelpie/tree/HEAD/skills/kelpie))
- **kelpie-deploy**: Put a new kelpie build in front of a running fleet: discover the daemon's unit and paths, back up the database, install every binary, restart, and prove it came back. Use when asked to deploy, ship, roll out, install, or restart kelpie or kelpied, when a fix is committed and the daemon is still serving the old code, after adding a migration, or when the running daemon and the working tree have diverged. Also use before concluding that a change is live. ([dcadenas/kelpie](https://github.com/dcadenas/kelpie/tree/HEAD/skills/kelpie-deploy))
- **kelpie-diagnose**: Read kelpie's durable record to settle what actually happened to a message, delivery, renew, or start — with timestamps rather than guesses. Use when an agent says it never received a prompt, a renew looks stuck or wrongly failed, a delivery says accepted but nothing arrived, an obligation is still open, a notice needs explaining, or a peer reports a kelpie bug. Also use before accepting any claim about kelpie behaviour that the database can confirm or refute. ([dcadenas/kelpie](https://github.com/dcadenas/kelpie/tree/HEAD/skills/kelpie-diagnose))

- **kenan-skill**: 名侦探柯南的推理思维框架与表达方式。基于 6 个维度、约 120 条带出处条目的调研， 提炼 6 个核心心智模型、10 条决策启发式和完整的表达 DNA。 用途：作为思维顾问，用柯南的视角拆解一个具体对象——一段话术、一个说法、一笔账、一套方案、 一个局——找出被掩盖的前提、说不通的地方，以及谁在受益。 当用户提到「名侦探柯南」「用柯南的视角」「柯南会怎么看」「柯南模式」「名侦探模式」「新一视角」「工藤新一」「conan perspective」时使用。 当用户带着一个具体说法或材料求解，措辞是「这话术哪里不对劲」「帮我拆一下这个局」「他是不是在骗我」 「这方案有没有猫腻」「谁在说谎」「这个理由站不住脚吧」「还有别的可能吗」时，也应触发。 英文触发：conan perspective / detective mode / break down this claim / conan mode。 只在指向一个具体说法、事件、材料或决策时才触发。 用户只单说「真相是什么」「帮我排除一下」「分析一下」而没给出对象时（写代码、debug、做表格、日常问答），不触发。 ([mingdui/kenan-skill](https://github.com/mingdui/kenan-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
